---

## US-022: Database migration: add inventory and financial tracking tables
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: supabase/migrations/00002_inventory_financial.sql, prd.json, scripts/ralph/prd.json
- Notes: Created migration with 2 new enums (item_status, listing_platform), 4 new tables (inventory_items, listings, sales, shipments). All tables have UUID PKs, proper FK constraints (CASCADE on delete, SET NULL for optional refs), indexes on all FKs and status columns, RLS policies enforcing ownership through the inventory_items -> user_id chain, and set_updated_at triggers on tables with updated_at columns. Sales table intentionally has no updated_at (immutable records). Shipments RLS uses a JOIN through sales to inventory_items for ownership verification.
- Timestamp: 2026-02-16
---

## US-023: TypeScript types for inventory and financial tables
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: src/types/database.ts, prd.json, scripts/ralph/prd.json
- Notes: Added ItemStatus (8 values) and ListingPlatform (8 values) enum types. Created Row, Insert, and Update types for inventory_items, listings, sales, and shipments tables. Updated Database interface with 4 new table entries in public.Tables and 2 new enum entries in public.Enums. All nullable SQL columns mapped to `T | null`. Insert types have optional fields for columns with DB defaults. Sales has no updated_at (immutable records per migration design). Typecheck and build pass clean.
- Timestamp: 2026-02-16

## US-024: Database migration: admin tables and roles
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: supabase/migrations/00003_admin_tables.sql, src/types/database.ts, prd.json, scripts/ralph/prd.json
- Notes: Created user_role enum (user/reviewer/admin/super_admin), added role column to users table (default 'user'). Three new tables: admin_audit_log (for tracking admin actions), human_reviews (for reviewer overrides on low-confidence grades), ai_prompt_versions (for tracking AI prompt iterations and accuracy). Created is_admin() and is_reviewer_or_admin() SECURITY DEFINER helper functions for RLS. RLS policies: audit log admin-only, human reviews admin+reviewer, prompt versions admin-only (full CRUD). Added indexes on all FKs, action/target_type columns, and users.role. TypeScript types updated with UserRole enum, role on UserRow/UserInsert, and Row/Insert/Update types for all 3 new tables plus Database interface entries.
- Timestamp: 2026-02-16

## US-026: Image upload utility with Supabase Storage
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: src/lib/storage.ts, prd.json, scripts/ralph/prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- Notes: Created storage utility module with three exported functions: uploadSubmissionImage (validates file type/size, uploads to {userId}/{submissionId}/{imageType}_{timestamp}.{ext}), getImageUrl (creates signed URLs with 1hr expiry), deleteSubmissionImage (removes files from bucket). Validates against MIME types (jpeg/png/webp) and 10MB size limit matching the DB bucket config. All errors throw with meaningful messages. Typecheck and build pass clean.
- Timestamp: 2026-02-16
---

## US-027: Client-side image compression and validation
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: src/lib/image-utils.ts, prd.json, scripts/ralph/prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- Notes: Created image-utils module with two exported functions: validateImage(file) checks MIME type (jpeg/png/webp), file size (10MB max), and resolution (min 1200x1200px), returning structured validation errors with user-friendly messages. compressImage(file, maxWidth, quality) uses canvas API to resize images (default max 2400px wide), compress (default 0.85 quality), and correct EXIF orientation by parsing the orientation tag from the first 64KB of JPEG files. Handles all 8 EXIF orientation values including dimension swaps for rotated images. Outputs WebP for JPEG/WebP inputs, preserves PNG format. Typecheck and build pass clean.
- Timestamp: 2026-02-16
---

## US-028: Edge function: JWT auth middleware
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: services/edge-functions/src/middleware/auth.ts, services/edge-functions/src/main.ts, prd.json, scripts/ralph/prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- Notes: Created Hono middleware that extracts Bearer token from Authorization header, validates it via supabase.auth.getUser(), and sets user and userId on the Hono context. Returns 401 JSON error for missing or invalid tokens. Applied to /api/grade/* routes in main.ts using app.use() path matching. Health and webhook routes remain unprotected as required. Uses createMiddleware from hono/factory with typed Variables for user and userId.
- Timestamp: 2026-02-16
---

## US-029: Edge function: rate limiting middleware
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: services/edge-functions/src/middleware/rate-limit.ts, services/edge-functions/src/main.ts, prd.json, scripts/ralph/prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- Notes: Created rateLimiter(maxRequests, windowMs) factory function that returns Hono middleware. Uses in-memory Map keyed by userId from auth context. Tracks request count and window reset time per user. Returns 429 with Retry-After header and JSON error body when limit exceeded. Includes periodic cleanup of expired entries (every 5 minutes). Adds standard X-RateLimit-Limit, X-RateLimit-Remaining, and X-RateLimit-Reset headers to all responses. Applied to /api/grade/* routes after auth middleware with default 60 requests per 60 seconds. Skips rate limiting if no userId is available in context.
- Timestamp: 2026-02-16
---

## US-030: Photo upload UI component with drag-and-drop
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: src/components/submission/photo-upload.tsx, prd.json, scripts/ralph/prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- Notes: Created PhotoUpload component with 8 upload slots (front, back, label, detail required; detail 2, defect 1-3 optional). Each slot shows icon (Shirt/Tag/Search/AlertTriangle from lucide-react), label, required indicator, and description. Supports drag-and-drop (onDragOver/onDrop) and click-to-select (hidden file input). Shows image preview with hover-reveal remove button. Validates via existing validateImage (type/size/resolution) and compresses via compressImage before emitting. Emits onChange with PhotoUploadItem[] ({file, imageType, preview}). Responsive grid: 2 cols mobile, 4 cols desktop. Used Map<string, SlotState> instead of Record for strict TypeScript compatibility. Processing spinner shown during validation/compression.
- Timestamp: 2026-02-16

## US-033: Submission creation API endpoint
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: services/edge-functions/src/routes/grade.ts, supabase/migrations/00004_increment_grades_function.sql, prd.json, scripts/ralph/prd.json
- Notes: Fully implemented POST /api/grade/submit endpoint with multipart form data parsing. Validates garment_type, garment_category, title (required), and image files (requires front, back, label, and at least 1 detail image). Checks user's grade usage against plan limits (free:5, starter:50, professional:500, enterprise:unlimited) with monthly reset logic. Creates submission record with status 'pending', uploads each image to Supabase storage under {userId}/{submissionId}/{imageType}_{timestamp}.{ext}, creates submission_image records with display_order. Increments grades_used_this_month via Postgres RPC function (new migration 00004) or resets counter if month rolled over. Returns 201 with { submissionId, status } on success, 403 with upgrade message if limit exceeded, 400 with detailed validation errors for invalid input. Also implemented GET /status/:id endpoint scoped to authenticated user. Includes cleanup logic on failure (deletes uploaded images and submission record).
- Timestamp: 2026-02-16
---

## US-034: Claude Vision API integration for per-image analysis
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: services/edge-functions/src/lib/ai-grading.ts (new), prd.json, scripts/ralph/prd.json
- Notes: Created ai-grading.ts module exporting analyzeImage(imageUrl, imageType, garmentType) function and typed interfaces (PerImageAnalysis, DetectedIssue, ConditionSignal, FactorScores). Uses Anthropic SDK v0.24.1 with base64 image source (parseImageInput helper handles data URI and raw base64 formats). System prompt establishes expert clothing condition assessor role with grading scale and 5 weighted factors. User prompt includes image-type-specific context (front/back/label/detail/defect) and garment-type-specific criteria (tops/bottoms/outerwear/dresses/footwear/accessories). Requests structured JSON with detected_issues[], condition_signals[], and estimated_scores per factor. Response parsing strips markdown code fences, validates structure, and clamps scores to 1.0-10.0 range. Error handling covers timeouts, rate limits, and parse failures with contextual error messages. Logs API usage (input/output tokens, latency) for monitoring.
- Timestamp: 2026-02-16
---

## US-035: Claude Vision API: composite grading from all images
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: services/edge-functions/src/lib/ai-grading.ts, prd.json, scripts/ralph/prd.json
- Notes: Added compositeGrade(perImageResults[], garmentInfo) function to ai-grading.ts. Exports new types: CompositeGradeResult, GarmentInfo, DefectFound. Sends all per-image analyses as structured JSON input to Claude with comprehensive composite system prompt including full grade tier definitions (NWT through Salvage with score ranges and descriptions). Applies factor weights (fabric 30%, structural 25%, cosmetic 20%, functional 15%, odor 10%) and recalculates weighted average server-side for authoritative scoring. Rounds overall_score to nearest 0.5 increment. Derives grade_tier deterministically from calculated score via scoreToGradeTier(). Validates and clamps all factor scores (1.0-10.0), confidence (0.0-1.0). Sets needs_human_review flag when confidence < 0.75. Includes prompt_version field ("composite_v1") referencing ai_prompt_versions concept. Validates defects_found array entries for required fields and valid severity values. Comprehensive error handling for timeouts, rate limits, and parse failures with API usage logging.
- Timestamp: 2026-02-16
---

## US-036: Grade calculation and report creation pipeline
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: services/edge-functions/src/lib/grading-pipeline.ts (new), prd.json, scripts/ralph/prd.json
- Notes: Created processSubmission(submissionId) orchestration function that runs the full grading pipeline. Fetches submission record and validates status is 'pending', sets status to 'processing'. Fetches associated submission_images, downloads each from Supabase Storage, converts Blob to base64 data URI with correct MIME type detection from file extension. Runs analyzeImage() on all images in parallel via Promise.all. Runs compositeGrade() with per-image results and garment info. Creates grade_report record with all factor scores, AI summary, confidence score, model version, and a UUID certificate_id generated via crypto.randomUUID(). Builds detailed_notes object from per-image issue/signal summaries plus defects summary. Updates submission status to 'completed' on success. Full try/catch wraps entire pipeline — on failure, logs error and updates submission status to 'failed' (with nested try/catch to handle status update failures). Comprehensive console logging at each pipeline stage with timing metrics.
- Timestamp: 2026-02-16
---

## US-040: Stripe checkout session creation for grade payments
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: services/edge-functions/src/routes/payments.ts (new), services/edge-functions/src/main.ts, scripts/ralph/prd.json, prd.json
- Notes: Created new payments route file with POST /checkout-session endpoint. Accepts { submissionId, tier } JSON body. Three tiers: standard ($2.99/299 cents), premium ($7.99/799 cents), express ($12.99/1299 cents). Validates tier is valid, validates user owns the submission via Supabase query. Creates Stripe Checkout Session with mode:payment, price_data with product name/description, and metadata containing submission_id, user_id, and tier for webhook processing. Success URL redirects to /dashboard/submissions/:id?payment=success, cancel URL to /dashboard/submissions?payment=cancelled. Returns { sessionId, url }. Registered route in main.ts at /api/payments with auth middleware applied.
- Timestamp: 2026-02-16
---

## US-041: Stripe webhook handling for payment events
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: services/edge-functions/src/routes/webhooks.ts, scripts/ralph/prd.json, prd.json
- Notes: Replaced stub webhook handler with full Stripe webhook implementation. Verifies webhook signature using stripe.webhooks.constructEvent with STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET env var. Handles 4 event types: (1) checkout.session.completed — reads submission_id/user_id from session metadata, marks submission payment_status as paid, triggers grading pipeline fire-and-forget; (2) customer.subscription.created/updated — finds user by stripe_customer_id, maps subscription to plan tier via metadata/lookup_key/price amount, updates user plan; (3) customer.subscription.deleted — downgrades user to free plan; (4) invoice.payment_failed — logs event with user context for debugging. Returns 200 for all handled events (including handler errors to prevent retries), 400 for signature verification failures. All events logged with [Webhook] prefix.
- Timestamp: 2026-02-16
---

## US-064: Edge function: API key generation and validation
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: services/edge-functions/src/middleware/api-key-auth.ts (new), services/edge-functions/src/main.ts, prd.json, scripts/ralph/prd.json
- Notes: Created api-key-auth.ts middleware that validates API keys from the X-API-Key header. Hashes the provided key with SHA-256 and matches against stored key_hash in api_keys table. Validates key format (gt_sk_ prefix + 64 hex chars = 70 chars total). Checks if key is expired via expires_at timestamp. Updates last_used_at as fire-and-forget (non-blocking) on successful validation. Sets user and userId on Hono context from the key's user_id, matching the same interface as the JWT auth middleware. Updated CORS configuration to allow X-API-Key header. POST/DELETE routes and key generation logic already existed from US-063.
- Timestamp: 2026-02-18
---

## US-065: Public REST API: grade submission and retrieval
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: services/edge-functions/src/routes/api-v1.ts (new), services/edge-functions/src/main.ts, prd.json, scripts/ralph/prd.json
- Notes: Created api-v1.ts route file with 3 endpoints versioned under /api/v1/. POST /api/v1/grades accepts JSON body with garment info and images array (each image has image_type + url or base64), validates required fields and image types, downloads/decodes images, uploads to Supabase Storage, creates submission, triggers grading pipeline, returns 202 with { data: { id, status }, error: null, meta: null }. GET /api/v1/grades/:id returns full submission info with grade report (all factor scores, AI summary, certificate_id) for completed grades. GET /api/v1/grades returns paginated list with ?page=&limit=&status= query params, includes grade summary for completed items, returns meta with page/limit/total/total_pages/has_next/has_prev. All responses follow consistent { data, error, meta } JSON structure. Authenticated via X-API-Key header using apiKeyAuthMiddleware. Rate limited at 100 req/min via existing rateLimiter middleware. Plan limit checking and usage increment reuse same logic as internal grade routes.
- Timestamp: 2026-02-18
---

  - Encryption uses crypto-aes.ts encryptToken/decryptToken (AES-256-GCM, AAD=user_id),
    the canonical helper that superseded US-119's FLIPDESK_INTERNAL_JOB_SECRET approach
    (same one google-photos + eBay already use). The AC's mention of the job-secret key
    is stale.
  - Mounting order matters: /api/flipdesk/google/photos is mounted (and its public
    callback wired) BEFORE /api/flipdesk/google; the new google routes are listed
    per-path in middleware (not a /google/* wildcard) so they never shadow the public
    Photos callback.
  - Scopes: drive.file (per-file, NOT full drive) + spreadsheets, access_type=offline +
    prompt=consent so Google always returns a refresh token (even on reconnect).
  - DEVIATION from AC#4: "Use existing sheet" is a paste-link/id input validated
    server-side via Sheets API (clear 403 when the app lacks drive.file access to that
    file) rather than the full gapi Drive Picker. The Picker needs a client-side Google
    API key that isn't provisioned yet (the story's original blocker). "Create new sync
    sheet" is the fully-wired primary path; the Picker can be layered in later behind a
    VITE_GOOGLE_API_KEY without touching the server contract.
  - "Migration 00017" in the AC was a stale story-era number; next free migration is 00131.
  - First `npm run build` hit a transient esbuild vite:dep-scan error on an UNRELATED file
    (use-remove-bg, which exists and was unmodified); a clean re-run exits 0.

## US-147: Google Sheets 2-way live sync (push + pull + conflict resolution)
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: supabase/migrations/00132_google_sheet_sync.sql, services/edge-functions/src/lib/sheet-sync.ts (new), services/edge-functions/src/lib/google-sheets-api.ts (new), services/edge-functions/src/routes/flipdesk-google-sync.ts (new), services/edge-functions/src/routes/flipdesk-google.ts, services/edge-functions/src/main.ts, services/edge-functions/src/lib/schema-version.ts, services/edge-functions/src/tests/rls-guard_test.ts, services/edge-functions/src/tests/google-sheet-sync_test.ts (new), src/hooks/use-google-sheets.ts, src/pages/flipdesk/marketplaces-google.tsx, LAUNCH_CHECKLIST.md
- Notes:
  * Conflict detection needs a 3rd copy of the data: google_sheet_sync_state stores per-field values as of the last sync ("base"). sheet!=base & db==base -> pull; db!=base & sheet==base -> push; both changed -> conflict (FlipDesk wins, row flagged in Conflicts tab with Accept Sheet dropdown applied on next run).
  * KEY DESIGN DECISION: /sync/push and /sync/pull both run the SAME full bidirectional merge. A push-only pass that rewrites whole rows would clobber un-pulled sheet edits; since push needs to read the sheet anyway (row mapping by hidden flipdesk_id), the merge is free.
  * Field allow-list enforced structurally: only ColumnDef.writable columns can ever produce pulls (title, brand, size, sku, status, acquired_price=cost_basis, target_price, condition_notes=notes). Invalid values (bad enum, negative price, blanked title) are reverted in the sheet + logged to Sync Log.
  * Values compared after normalization ("$12.50" in sheet == numeric 12.5 from PostgREST); writes use RAW + reads use UNFORMATTED_VALUE to keep round-trips stable.
  * Two CI guards fired on the new migration (good!): schema-version_test wants EXPECTED_SCHEMA_VERSION bumped in the same commit, and rls-guard_test requires classifying any zero-policy table in SERVICE_ROLE_ONLY.
  * Listings/Sales tenant-scoping goes through inventory_items!inner(user_id) since those tables carry no user_id (US-268 pattern).
  * Docker was down so the local supabase db reset lane was skipped (simple CREATE TABLE migration; CI db-migrations.yml will verify).
- Timestamp: 2026-06-11 09:35

## US-339: Original-image + EXIF retention pipeline (forensic/provenance enabler)
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: src/lib/image-utils.ts (extractExif + pure-TS EXIF/TIFF parser), src/components/submission/photo-upload.tsx (capture exif + originalFile on PhotoUploadItem), src/pages/new-submission.tsx (submit exif_metadata parallel array + optional original_images, disclosure note), src/types/database.ts (ImageExifMetadata, exif/original_storage_path/phash on SubmissionImageRow+Insert), services/edge-functions/src/routes/grade.ts (sanitizeExif + RETAIN_ORIGINAL_IMAGES gate + store exif jsonb + optional original retention + cleanup), supabase/migrations/00137_submission_image_exif.sql, src/pages/legal/privacy.tsx (EXIF/GPS disclosure)
- Notes: EXIF is read from the ORIGINAL file BEFORE compressImage's canvas re-encode strips it. Pure-TS parser (no dependency, mirrors existing getExifOrientation) reads JPEG APP1/TIFF IFD0 (make/model/software/dateTime/orientation) + ExifIFD (DateTimeOriginal/lensModel) + GPS IFD (lat/lon w/ ref signs). Non-JPEG → null (phones shoot JPEG/HEIC; HEIC not accepted). Server sanitizes/bounds EXIF (string caps, GPS range-checked) → submission_images.exif jsonb. Original retention is double-gated: client VITE_RETAIN_ORIGINALS + server RETAIN_ORIGINAL_IMAGES (default OFF, so fast compressed path is unchanged — no regression). Originals stored EXIF-INTACT (not stripped — forensic intent) in the same PRIVATE bucket + tenant-scoped owner folder, signed-URL-only access, never public/buyer-facing. Privacy disclosed in /privacy + upload step. Build/tsc/lint + deno check/lint all pass. Enables US-340/US-341.
- Timestamp: 2026-06-11

## US-341: Server-side forensic/ML manipulation pass on original images
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: services/edge-functions/src/lib/forensics.ts (new), services/edge-functions/src/tests/forensics_test.ts (new), services/edge-functions/src/lib/grading-pipeline.ts, services/edge-functions/src/lib/schema-version.ts, supabase/migrations/00139_forensic_analysis.sql (new), src/types/database.ts, prd.json
- Notes: Decoder-free structural forensic layer (no native image codec in Deno edge): JPEG marker walk -> Adobe APP14 marker, progressive scan, DQT count + luminance quant-table quality estimate (estimateJpegQuality inverts the Annex-K scaling), plus EXIF editor-software + provenance-absent tells. Pure scoring (scoreImageForensics) is FP-guarded so NO single benign edit (re-save/Adobe export/progressive/absent-EXIF) crosses the 0.6 suspect threshold — flagging needs corroboration. aggregateForensics -> overall tamper likelihood; fuseTamperSignals combines forensic+vision via noisy-OR (corroboration => max confidence penalty 0.3 + review), and when no original was retained the fusion is exactly the vision signal (compressed-only path unchanged). Orchestration (runForensicPass) runs ONLY on retained originals (US-339 original_storage_path), bounded by FORENSIC_MAX_IMAGES (default 6) + FORENSIC_ANALYSIS kill-switch, never throws. Wired into grading-pipeline after composite: lowers confidence, sets needs_human_review, adds detailed_notes.forensic_analysis, flags moderation on forensic-suspected, persists grade_reports.forensic_analysis (migration 00139, omitted from public_grade_reports view). Pixel-domain ELA/PRNU/copy-move/inpainting deferred to a GPU/worker tier feeding the same ForensicFeatures+scoring core. Bumped EXPECTED_SCHEMA_VERSION 00135->00139 (was already stale behind concurrent 00136-00138). 21 unit tests pass; full edge suite 700+ pass; frontend tsc + build clean.

## US-405: Eliminate the per-row N+1 in the eBay sync
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: services/edge-functions/src/routes/flipdesk-ebay.ts, prd.json
- Notes: doListingsPull() previously did, per offer/legacy listing, a SELECT (most-recent listing) + an INSERT/UPDATE + a status flip — ~thousands of sequential PostgREST round-trips on a large seller. Refactor: (1) pre-load all of this user's existing eBay listings (most-recent per item) into an `existingListingByItem` Map via ONE inner-join query (tenant-scoped on inventory_items.user_id), replacing the per-row SELECT in BOTH the modern offers loop and the legacy Trading-API loop. (2) Accumulate every write in memory and flush as a handful of bulk calls before the orders pass: a single `listings.upsert(onConflict:"id")` over full uniform `ListingWrite` rows — new rows get client-generated `crypto.randomUUID()` ids so a later pass (and the flush) addresses the same row instead of double-inserting, and every row carries all NOT NULL columns so the upsert's INSERT arm is valid for both new and existing; a single `flipdesk_ebay_listings.upsert(onConflict:"user_id,ebay_item_id")` over an `orphanByEbayId` map (de-duped by ebay_item_id so the bulk upsert can't "affect a row a second time"); and the inventory status flips collapsed to TWO `.in("id",[...])` updates — a notify set (modern offers, fires the listing_live notification once on the real transition via `.select()`) and a silent set (legacy). Notify flip runs first so an item active on both surfaces still notifies. (3) Catalog patches deliberately kept as per-row UPDATEs: inventory_items has NOT NULL cols (status, etc.) the upsert INSERT arm would violate or clobber, and patches are gated fill-if-blank so steady-state syncs issue ~0 of them. Behavior preserved: US-148 source-of-truth pins + cross-source observations (computed from the pre-loaded snapshot before overwrite), US-465 orphan match_status preservation (column still omitted from the upsert), US-737 notifications, forward-only prep-status flips. Latent bug avoided: the old per-row INSERT spread a patch whose listing_price could be `undefined` (NOT NULL) when an offer had no price; the new ensurePendingListing defaults it to 0. Verified: `deno check src/main.ts`, `deno lint`, and the ebay-catalog-merge/ebay-order-lines/tenant-isolation/reconciliation-shape test files all green. Live-DB benchmark (AC3) not runnable here (no integration env); the round-trip count for a 1k-offer steady-state re-sync drops from ~4k to ~5.
- Timestamp: 2026-06-11
---

## US-406: Bound eBay API fan-out + honor rate limits
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: services/edge-functions/src/lib/retry.ts, services/edge-functions/src/lib/ebay-client.ts, services/edge-functions/src/tests/retry_test.ts, prd.json
- Notes: Three-part fix for the listAllOffers fan-out (one getOffers per SKU, no cap/backoff). (1) Retry-After honored: ebay-client fetchAuthedOnce parses the response Retry-After header (whole-seconds or HTTP-date) via parseRetryAfter() and attaches retryAfterMs to the thrown error; withRetry (retry.ts) waits exactly that long when present (capped at maxRetryAfterMs=30s so a hostile/misconfigured header can not stall a sync), else the existing full-jitter backoff. (2) retry.ts exports isRateLimitError() (429 / quota-message only, distinct from generic 5xx) and retryAfterMs(). (3) listAllOffers bounds the SKU fan-out at MAX_OFFERS_FANOUT_PER_SYNC=2000 with OFFERS_FANOUT_CONCURRENCY=5 (tuned to Sell-API limits): when allItems exceeds the ceiling it processes the prefix and pushes a documented partial-continuation warning (next incremental sync continues from the stable inventory_item order); when a per-SKU call exhausts retries on a 429 it sets rateLimited, stops the fan-out, and pushes a rate-limit-SPECIFIC partial warning instead of silently truncating. Both warnings flow into doListingsPull errors[], which already flips the run to status partial (flipdesk-ebay.ts:2156). eBay has no bulk getOffers (fan-out is forced by the API); the bulk inventory_item listing is already paginated 100/page. Verified: deno check src/main.ts + ebay-client.ts + retry.ts, deno lint clean, retry_test.ts 11/11 pass. Frontend untouched. Live 429 exhaustion not reproducible without an eBay integration env.
- Timestamp: 2026-06-11

## US-407: Move long-running sync/publish/autolister to a durable job queue
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: services/edge-functions/src/routes/flipdesk-ebay.ts, services/edge-functions/src/tests/publish-due-batch_test.ts, services/edge-functions/COOLIFY.md, prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- Notes: Most of this story was already satisfied by prior durability work — sync via flipdesk_sync_runs claim + stuck-reaper (US-456), autolister via batches/jobs claim+heartbeat+reclaim cron (US-525/526), publish via per-row publish_claimed_at claim + stale reclaim (US-528). The remaining gap was AC#2: publish-due scanned up to 100 drafts serially (each = several eBay calls) inside a 240s job-lock lease, so a large backlog could blow past the lease and overlap the next tick. Fixed by bounding the scan to a small batch (publishBatchLimit(), default 15, overridable via PUBLISH_DUE_BATCH_LIMIT, clamped to 100); fetch batchLimit+1 to detect remaining backlog and report response.more; ordered by scheduled_publish_at so the oldest drains first. The 5-min cron drains larger backlogs over ticks. Also registered ebay-sync-reaper in COOLIFY.md scheduled tasks (it was mounted + in LAUNCH_CHECKLIST but absent from COOLIFY.md) so stranded running sync rows are reaped autonomously, not only on a user's next pull. Edge-only + docs change (no frontend); deno check + lint clean, 5 new unit tests pass.
- Timestamp: 2026-06-11

## US-448: Verify focus management for custom overlays
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: src/lib/a11y/focus-trap.ts (new), src/hooks/use-focus-trap.ts (new), src/lib/a11y/__tests__/focus-trap.test.ts (new), src/components/certificate/image-lightbox.tsx, prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- Notes: Audit story. Surveyed every overlay named in the AC + a full grep for custom modal patterns (fixed inset-0 / role="dialog" / aria-modal). Findings: photo-editor-dialog uses Radix Dialog (focus trap, ESC, restore, background-inert all handled by Radix — compliant, no change); item-canvas, annotated-photo and disclosure-panel render INLINE (full-page editor / canvas display / Card) — they are not modal overlays so the trap criteria are N/A; cookie-consent is a non-modal bottom banner (intentionally no aria-modal). The ONE hand-rolled modal overlay is the certificate ImageLightbox. It already trapped Tab + ESC + restored focus, but its only background isolation was a body scroll-lock — it did NOT mark background content inert/aria-hidden (AC #2 gap), so a screen-reader virtual cursor could still wander out. Fix: extracted a reusable, framework-agnostic trapFocus(container,{onEscape,initialFocus}) in src/lib/a11y/focus-trap.ts that (1) focuses in, (2) walks the ancestor chain up to <body> marking every sibling inert+aria-hidden (isolates the overlay without needing a portal; idempotent/exact restore via remembered prior aria-hidden state), (3) locks body scroll, (4) traps Tab/Shift+Tab + pulls back escaped focus, (5) Esc -> onEscape, and returns a release() that undoes everything and refocuses the opener. useFocusTrap hook wraps it for React (keeps latest onEscape in a ref so the trap isn't re-armed each render). Refactored ImageLightbox onto the hook (close button gets initial focus; arrow-key gallery nav kept separate). 
- Gotchas: tsc -b (the project-references build that `npm run build` uses) is stricter than `tsc --noEmit` — it rejected the `let node` / `const parent = node.parentElement` loop with TS7022 (implicit-any self-reference) until both got explicit `: HTMLElement` annotations and the while-guard moved to `node.parentElement`. No @testing-library in repo, so the trap logic lives in a pure DOM lib fn tested directly against jsdom (10 tests: focus-in, initialFocus, inert+aria-hidden set/restore, Tab + Shift+Tab wrap, escaped-focus pull-back, Esc, focus restore, scroll lock; + getFocusable inert-subtree skip).
- Verification: npx tsc --noEmit clean, npm run build clean (tsc -b + vite + 28 prerendered routes), eslint clean on changed files, full vitest suite 495/495 pass (48 files).
- Timestamp: 2026-06-12

## US-472: Make eBay webhook/payout user-linkage reliable
- Status: COMPLETE

- Notes:
  * Connect reliability: getUserIdentityFromToken now retries (3x w/ backoff) on transient (5xx/429/network) failures so account_handle + external_account_id are captured reliably on first connect; a real 4xx is not retried. Refresh-path backfill unchanged (already hydrates nulls).
  * Stable-key matching: webhooks now resolve seller via shared extractEbayCandidates -> resolveEbayConnectionUserId, preferring external_account_id (stable eBay userId/payeeId/sellerId) then account_handle. Payout handler previously matched ONLY on handle.
  * No data loss: verified-but-unlinkable payout/order/return events are PARKED in new table ebay_pending_webhook_events (deduped by notificationId, RLS on, service-role only) instead of silently dropped. New drain cron handleEbayPendingWebhooksCron at POST /api/jobs/ebay-pending-webhooks (every 15min, job-secret + overlap lock) re-attempts linkage and replays linked events (payout->dedup ingest via shared ingestPayoutForUser, order/return->triggerEbaySyncForUser); dead-letters after 8 attempts (CSV fallback) with a captured warning.
  * Metric: webhook.dropped_no_handle on every parked drop (payout + order/return); plus webhook.pending_linked / webhook.pending_dead_lettered / webhook.pending_drain.
  * Pre-existing drift fixed: EXPECTED_SCHEMA_VERSION was 00145 while migrations already reached 00148 (concurrent agent); bumped to 00149 to satisfy schema-version_test.

## US-480: Compute the photo-reuse perceptual hash server-side
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed:
  * services/edge-functions/src/lib/perceptual-hash.ts (NEW) — dHashFromRgba (pure) + computePhashFromImage (decode + hash)
  * services/edge-functions/src/routes/grade.ts — stop trusting client "phashes"; recompute from stored cleanBytes
  * services/edge-functions/src/routes/api-v1.ts — compute + store server phash (public API now covered by reuse detection too)
  * services/edge-functions/deno.json + deno.lock — add @jsquash/webp for WebP decode
  * services/edge-functions/src/tests/perceptual-hash_test.ts (NEW) — 7 tests
- Notes:
  * Threat: submission_images.phash was a client-submitted field (grade.ts), trivially spoofable to dodge a match against a stolen/stock photo. Now the dHash is recomputed server-side from the validated/stored upload bytes; the client value is ignored (and the parsing removed). computePhashFromImage takes no client-hash param, so client influence is structurally impossible.
  * Decode: imagescript (already a dep) handles JPEG/PNG; @jsquash/webp (lazy dynamic import) handles WebP — the dominant client output (canvas.toBlob image/webp). magick_wasm needed explicit wasm wiring; jsquash decodes cleanly in Deno.
  * Algorithm mirrors the browser dHash (src/lib/image-utils.ts) exactly — 9x8 area-downscale, Rec.601 luma (0.299/0.587/0.114), horizontal-neighbour compare, MSB-first, 16 hex — so server hashes stay comparable to the client-computed hashes already stored. Pixel decode → pure dHashFromRgba (own area-average downscale), fully unit-testable without a decoder.
  * Best-effort: any decode/hash failure → null (matches the browser "" fallback), so hashing never blocks an upload; reuse detection simply skips that image.
  * Dockerfile pre-caches main.ts (static graph); jsquash is lazy/dynamic so it resolves at runtime on first WebP (consistent with imagescript's runtime wasm fetch). deno.lock updated; `deno cache --frozen src/main.ts` passes.
- Verification: deno test perceptual-hash_test.ts 7 passed/0 failed; deno check (grade, api-v1, perceptual-hash, test, main.ts) clean; deno lint 4 files clean; deno cache --frozen clean; frontend npx tsc --noEmit clean.
- Timestamp: 2026-06-12
---

## US-487: Rotate content-scheduler secret + retry content webhooks
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: services/edge-functions/src/lib/job-auth.ts, services/edge-functions/src/lib/content-webhook.ts, services/edge-functions/src/routes/content-scheduler.ts, services/edge-functions/src/tests/job-auth_test.ts, services/edge-functions/src/lib/schema-version.ts, services/edge-functions/.env.example, docs/KEY_ROTATION.md
- Notes: (1) Secret rotation: schedulerAuth now accepts CONTENT_INTERNAL_JOB_SECRET_OLD alongside the primary, mirroring the FlipDesk overlap-rotation pattern; verifyJobSecret accepts an array of candidates. (2) Signed timestamped requests: X-Internal-Job-Timestamp + X-Internal-Job-Signature = HMAC-SHA256(secret, "v1:<ts>:<METHOD>:<path>") with a 5-min skew window and a process-local single-use cache, so replays are rejected; a request presenting a signature that fails verification is rejected outright (no fallthrough to weaker auth). In-memory replay cache is sound because the edge service deploys as a single container. (3) Webhook durability: retry/backoff (0s/5s/30s) + content_webhook_log recording already existed; added terminal-failure alerting (metric + captureException + CONTENT_ALERT_WEBHOOK with MONITOR_ALERT_WEBHOOK fallback); failed payloads remain retryable via POST /webhooks/:logId/retry. (4) Settings loaded once per tick and threaded into publishDueScheduledPosts/runBlogTick (was re-querying content_settings per published post). GOTCHA fixed in passing: US-486 added migration 00151 without bumping EXPECTED_SCHEMA_VERSION (00150), which failed schema-version_test in the edge lane — bumped to 00151.
- Timestamp: 2026-06-12

## US-543: Add a dedicated tag-OCR ground-truth pass
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: services/edge-functions/src/lib/ai-tag-ocr.ts (new), services/edge-functions/src/lib/ai-listing.ts (tag-OCR pass + tagGroundTruth prompt block/rule + token/cost rollup + log marker), services/edge-functions/src/tests/ai-tag-ocr_test.ts (new, 7 tests)
- Notes: Brand/size/fiber were previously read implicitly from busy garment shots, so listings could hallucinate them. New ai-tag-ocr.ts runs a SEPARATE focused vision pass over ONLY tag/tag_2 photos (nothing else in frame) using a forced read_garment_tag tool that transcribes brand, size, fiber_content, style_code, RN# verbatim with per-field confidence. Pure normalizeTagOcr maps/clamps the tool output; pure mergeTagGroundTruth folds reads >= TAG_GROUND_TRUTH_MIN_CONFIDENCE (0.4) into knownFields (tag WINS over stored item values; fiber->material, style_code->style) and returns a tagGroundTruth subset. generateListingFields now emits a TAG GROUND TRUTH block + a hard system-prompt rule weighting it ABOVE the model's own visual inference. In generateListing the pass runs after photo load (step 2b), is non-fatal on API failure (falls back to implicit inference), and its tokens/cost roll into ai_enrichment_log; a tag_ground_truth marker is logged so brand/size accuracy can be measured against a labeled sample. Verified: deno check (ai-tag-ocr/ai-listing/test + src/main.ts), deno lint (3 files), deno test ai-tag-ocr 7/7. Edge-only change (Deno) — frontend tsc/build unaffected.

## US-544: Normalize brand/SKU + resolve style codes
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: services/edge-functions/src/lib/brand-normalize.ts (new), services/edge-functions/src/lib/ai-listing.ts (wire normalization into generateListing), services/edge-functions/src/tests/brand-normalize_test.ts (new, 16 tests)
- Notes: Brand was free text and comp search keyed on item.brand raw, so "Levis"/"THE NORTHFACE" silently dropped eBay's exact-match Brand:{...} aspect filter to zero and priced off the unfiltered category. New brand-normalize.ts is fully pure (no net/db): (1) canonicalizeBrand() maps a free-text brand against a curated alias table to eBay's canonical spelling (Levis->Levi's, tnf->The North Face, adidas stays lowercase, guess->GUESS) and passes unknown brands through cleaned (whitespace-collapsed, casing kept); (2) resolveStyleCode() turns a sneaker/streetwear style code into a canonical product via a curated CW2288-111->{Nike, Air Force 1 '07} table plus Nike/adidas/New Balance FORMAT inference, returning the authoritative brand + an EXACT comp query (the code itself) + auto-fillable Model/Department/Type specifics; it returns null for non-code "style" values (e.g. "Slim Fit") so they're left alone, and a sneaker brandHint salvages unrecognized-format codes; (3) applyCanonicalBrandAndStyle() forces the canonical Brand specific (replacing the model's value) and folds product aspects in ONLY when the resolved category's allowedAspects permits them and the model didn't already set a value (so we never push an aspect eBay would reject at publish). In ai-listing.generateListing: step 2c computes normalizedBrand (tag/knownFields brand wins over item column; styleResolution.brand outranks the text brand); the refine-pass text and BOTH comp paths (getRealizedComps + searchBrowseComps) now key on normalizedBrand, with the style code as the comp query when present; step 6b applies the canonical Brand + style aspects to item_specifics; step 9 also writes the canonical brand back to inventory_items.brand so future comps stay canonical. Verified: deno check (brand-normalize/ai-listing + full graph via src/main.ts), deno lint (3 files clean), deno test brand-normalize 16/16, ai-tag-ocr + listing-confidence still 13/13. (autolister-reliability fails only on an imagescript wasm fetch needing --allow-net — environmental, pre-existing, unrelated.) Edge-only Deno change — frontend tsc/build unaffected.

## US-557: Unify the AutoLister save paths
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: src/components/flipdesk/ebay-category-picker.tsx, src/pages/flipdesk/composer.tsx, src/pages/flipdesk/autolister-bulk-edit.tsx, prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- Notes: Reconciled three disconnected save targets into one coherent model. (1) The eBay category picker no longer has its own "Save eBay specifics" button — it lifts its live aspect map to the parent via a new onAspectsChange callback. (2) The composer's single "Save draft" is now the one Save: it writes the picked aspects to BOTH listings.item_specifics_override (the per-listing canonical copy publish reads first) AND inventory_items.ebay_aspects + ebay_category_id (the fallback mirror) in the same save, so they never diverge and aspect edits can't vanish by skipping a separate save (AC1, AC2). Picker mount is gated on listingLoading/ebayMappingLoading so it seeds from real saved values, never a transient empty map that would persist as a wipe. (3) Bulk-grid saveAll switched from Promise.all-with-rollback to Promise.allSettled per concurrency chunk: only rows that actually committed are marked clean, failed rows stay dirty with edits intact, and there's no blanket query invalidate on partial failure (which previously refetched and discarded all dirty rows) (AC3). Publish resolution (listing.item_specifics_override ?? item.ebay_aspects) unchanged — both stores now stay in sync. tsc --noEmit, npm run build, and eslint all pass.
- Timestamp: 2026-06-12

## US-559: Move bulk publish to a durable server-side batch endpoint
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: supabase/migrations/00156_publish_batch_schema.sql (new), services/edge-functions/src/routes/flipdesk-autolister.ts, services/edge-functions/src/main.ts, services/edge-functions/src/tests/publish-batch_test.ts (new), src/hooks/use-autolister.ts, LAUNCH_CHECKLIST.md, services/edge-functions/COOLIFY.md, prd.json
- Notes: Replaced the client loop over /listings/push with a durable, server-owned bulk-publish batch that mirrors the AutoLister generation batch model one-for-one. Migration 00156 adds listing_publish_batches + listing_publish_jobs (reusing the existing listing_generation_status/job_status enums, same RLS + updated_at-trigger pattern as 00052). New edge endpoints in flipdesk-autolister.ts: POST /publish-batch (verifies ownership, gates the whole run against the active-listing cap up front with delta = items not already 'listed', creates batch + one job/item, 202 + batch_id, processes in background), GET /publish-batch/:id (batch + per-item status/error/listing_url for polling), POST /publish-batch/:id/{retry-failed,resume}, and handlePublishBatchReclaimCron mounted at /api/jobs/publish-batch-reclaim (job-secret gated, sweeps abandoned batches). processPublishBatch mirrors processBatch (atomic claim of pending/stale-running jobs, PUBLISH_CONCURRENCY=3, per-item withTimeout, finalize/heartbeat each slice + in finally) but wraps the proven publishItemForOwner — which is idempotent via publishOrAdoptOffer (US-464), so a re-run adopts a live offer rather than minting a duplicate (AC3) — and deliberately adds NO second retry layer (the old client loop's retry compounded the server's withRetry, per the story notes). Survives tab-close because the 202 closes the connection and the worker runs server-side; the reclaim cron resumes across a container restart (AC2). Per-item validation/blockers flow through assemblePublishContext inside publishItemForOwner and are surfaced into the job's error (publishFailureMessage: blockers > detail > error > generic). Rate budget centralized in the single bounded worker (AC1/AC2). Frontend useBulkPublish keeps its { run, results, running } public surface untouched (drafts cockpit unchanged) but now starts the server batch and polls GET /publish-batch/:id, mapping jobs → per-row results; removed the client-side markListingOutcome write (server owns synced_to_ebay_at / publish_error now) and the now-unused supabase import. Registered the new reclaim cron in LAUNCH_CHECKLIST.md + COOLIFY.md (*/5 * * * *). Verification: npx tsc --noEmit, npm run build, eslint (frontend) all clean; deno check + deno lint + deno test (publish-batch_test.ts, 4 passing) + frozen-lockfile (edge) all clean.

## US-560: Add a quantity-aware relist endpoint
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: services/edge-functions/src/routes/flipdesk-ebay.ts, prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- Notes: Added POST /listings/:id/relist. Verifies ownership via loadListingOwned (tenant-safe), clamps the requested replenish quantity to >=1 (default 1), enforces the active-listing cap (mirrors /listings/push), writes listings.quantity, then calls publishItemForOwner({relist:true}). That path reuses the existing SKU offer (createOffer -> already-exists -> syncExistingOffer, which now refreshes availableQuantity) and re-publishes via publishOrAdoptOffer, which adopts an already-live listing so retries are idempotent. ## US-571: Fix in-memory cache coherence before enabling multi-replica
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: services/edge-functions/src/lib/coherent-cache.ts (new), supabase/migrations/00162_cache_coherence.sql (new), services/edge-functions/src/tests/coherent-cache_test.ts (new), services/edge-functions/src/lib/ebay-client.ts, services/edge-functions/src/lib/gsc-client.ts, services/edge-functions/src/lib/ai-grading.ts, services/edge-functions/src/lib/grading-eval.ts, services/edge-functions/src/lib/crypto-aes.ts, services/edge-functions/src/lib/schema-version.ts, services/edge-functions/src/routes/admin-grading.ts, prd.json
- Notes: P0 multi-replica coherence. Root cause: 4 module-level (per-process) caches break when scaled to >1 replica. New coherent-cache.ts gives two Postgres-backed primitives (no Redis), table edge_shared_cache + bump_cache_signal/get_cache_signal RPCs (migration 00162). (1) createSharedTokenCache: eBay app token + GSC token now live in a cluster-shared, AES-GCM-encrypted-at-rest (AAD=cache_key) value store with expiry — a replica reads the shared row before minting, so the cluster mints ~once/TTL-window instead of once/replica (brief mint race is harmless). Key carries eBay env so sandbox/prod don't cross. (2) createVersionedCache: grading-prompt cache is version-stamped — invalidatePromptCache() bumps the shared signal on prompt activate (grading-eval.activatePromptVersion) AND deactivate (admin-grading route), and readers cheaply poll the signal (micro-cached ~5s) so an eval-gated activation (defends migration 00050) takes effect on every replica within ~5s, not the old 60s data TTL. AES-key cache (crypto-aes.ts) deliberately left per-process + documented: env-derived, identical on all replicas, changes only on redeploy=restart, so it can't go stale. All store calls fail-safe (token caches fall back to local minting; versioned cache keeps serving last value within fallbackTtlMs) so a DB blip never fails a request. Verified: 9 new unit tests incl. cross-replica propagation (two cache instances over one shared store: after replica A activates, replica B picks up NEW immediately) and encrypted-at-rest round-trip; full edge suite 907 pass; RPCs exercised against real Postgres (0 default, monotonic bump, read-back). schema-version bumped 00161->00162 (sync-guard test). Follow-ups OUT OF SCOPE (not in AC): ai-listing.ts listing-prompt cache + apns.ts APNs JWT have the same per-process shape and could adopt these primitives later.
- Timestamp: 2026-06-12

## US-573: Load-test grade + autolister paths and right-size / autoscale the edge container
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: services/edge-functions/src/lib/grading-capacity.ts (new), services/edge-functions/src/lib/grading-pipeline.ts, services/edge-functions/src/routes/health.ts, services/edge-functions/docker-compose.coolify.yml, services/edge-functions/.env.example, services/edge-functions/src/tests/grading-capacity_test.ts (new), services/edge-functions/src/tests/health_test.ts, scripts/ops/loadtest-grading.mjs (new), CAPACITY.md (new), SCALING.md, prd.json
- Notes: P0 ops/perf. Root risk: grading-pipeline.ts step 3 buffers every submission's photos as in-process base64 data URIs held resident across vision analysis, and grades are kicked fire-and-forget from several places with NO submission-level bound -> N concurrent grades can OOM the 1G container. Fix has 4 parts. (1) grading-capacity.ts adds a process-wide semaphore withImageBufferSlot() (cap GRADING_MAX_CONCURRENT_PIPELINES, default 6) wrapping the download->base64->analyze block; queued pipelines hold only a closure + their grading lease (US-569), not base64, so peak resident base64 is bounded (~6x worst-case ~100MB ~= 600MB). Distinct from ai-limiter AI_MAX_CONCURRENCY which bounds Claude CALLS, not buffering. (2) Right-sized docker-compose.coolify.yml deploy.resources mem 1G->2G (reservation 256M->512M, CPU unchanged 1.5 — base64 is CPU-light), exported EDGE_MEMORY_LIMIT_MB=2048 so the app knows the limit. (3) New GET /health/metrics (dependency-free, unauth, safe to sample): summarizeMemory(Deno.memoryUsage(), limit) -> rss/heap MB + rss_pct_of_limit + headroom + pressure (ok<70 / elevated>=70 scale-out line / critical>=85). (4) scripts/ops/loadtest-grading.mjs drives grade/autolister at concurrency while sampling /health/metrics, FAILs if peak RSS>=80% of limit (the no-OOM AC gate; 80 deliberately above the 70 scale-out line). CAPACITY.md documents the base64 memory math, the bound, right-sized limits, the load test, and the memory/CPU scale-out rule + 30% headroom target (ties to US-501 replicas); cross-linked from SCALING.md. Verification: deno check src/main.ts clean; deno lint clean; new tests grading-capacity_test.ts (3, incl. cap-never-exceeded + slot-released-on-throw) + summarizeMemory cases in health_test.ts -> 11 pass; grading-durability_test.ts 13 pass (pipeline refactor didn't regress). styleHint hoisted out of the new closure (also used by composite grade). MANUAL pre-launch gate: run loadtest-grading.mjs against staging at launch-target concurrency for grade + autolister, record peak RSS% in CAPACITY.md as AC-4 sign-off.
- Timestamp: 2026-06-12

Fed into TanStack Query refetchInterval as `visible ? <ms> : false` for the three query-based pollers (notification-center, admin/dashboard, admin/system) — toggling the boolean re-evaluates query options so the poll timer is torn down on hide and rescheduled on return to foreground (refetchOnWindowFocus also fires an immediate refresh). Notification poll widened 30s->60s since realtime postgres_changes INSERTs are the primary delivery path and the poll is only a reconnect fallback. Submission-detail's raw 5s setInterval (active only while a grade is processing/pending) is now gated on `visible` and additionally calls refetchData() once immediately when becoming visible so a returning tab catches up; realtime still delivers status independently. Net effect: a hidden tab does ZERO background polling on all four surfaces (was 2/min notif, 1-2/min admin pages, 12/min in-flight submission detail). Added a 2-case unit test for the hook (visible start; hide->false->visible->true round trip via Object.defineProperty + visibilitychange dispatch). ## US-577: Add an edge-cache strategy for dynamic public SSR surfaces
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: functions/_shared/blog-render.ts, functions/cert/[id].ts, functions/verified/[handle].ts, functions/blog/[[path]].ts, services/edge-functions/src/lib/cloudflare-purge.ts, services/edge-functions/src/routes/admin-disputes.ts, services/edge-functions/src/routes/admin-grading.ts, services/edge-functions/src/routes/verified.ts, src/test/edge-cache.test.ts (new), docs/SEO_PERFORMANCE.md, prd.json
- Notes: P2 perf/SEO. The three SSR surfaces (cert/[id], verified/[handle], blog) already emitted SSR_CACHE_CONTROL, but Cloudflare Pages Functions are NOT colo-cached by default — s-maxage only governs downstream caches, so each crawler/share hit re-rendered. Added withEdgeCache() in blog-render.ts wrapping each render in the Cache API (caches.default): keyed on origin+pathname (query ignored so utm share links collapse to one entry), stores only GET 200 public responses (skips the blog `preview` private/no-store route + errors), emits `x-gt-cache: HIT|MISS` for verification, puts via waitUntil so the MISS isn't blocked, and degrades to a plain render when `caches` is absent (local wrangler). Refactored each onRequestGet into `withEdgeCache(context, () => render(context))` with the existing body extracted into a named async render fn. Purge-on-change: extended cloudflare-purge.ts with buildCertPurgeFiles (cert HTML + og/badge/slab, all of which encode the score) + buildSellerPurgeFiles (verified HTML + og card), plus best-effort wrappers purgeCertificateCache/purgeSellerProfileCache that short-circuit with no DB hit + no-op when CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN/ZONE_ID are unset and never throw. Wired into the score-change/edit write paths: admin-disputes resolve (when a grade is adjusted), admin-grading review adjust, and verified PUT /profile (purges both new + old handle on a rename). Blog publish/edit purge was already wired (content-blog.ts / content-scheduler.ts). GOTCHA: Cloudflare's cache.put rejects no-store responses and the colo won't cache a Function response off Cache-Control alone — the Cache API is what makes the HIT real and verifiable. Verification: src/test/edge-cache.test.ts 6 pass (MISS->HIT, query-string collapse, private/no-store skip, error skip, no-caches fallback, non-GET skip); tsc -b clean; functions tsc clean (only the pre-existing condition-index TS2345); deno check src/main.ts + deno lint on the 4 changed routes clean; npm run build + prerender green; full vitest 532 pass (the 6 grid-image-lazy/US-413 failures are pre-existing and unrelated). curl x-gt-cache HIT recipe + purge-trigger table documented in docs/SEO_PERFORMANCE.md.

## US-578: Define edge log retention + trace sampling/cost budget
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: services/edge-functions/src/lib/observability.ts, services/edge-functions/src/middleware/access-log.ts, services/edge-functions/docker-compose.coolify.yml, services/edge-functions/.env.example, services/edge-functions/OBSERVABILITY.md (new), services/edge-functions/src/tests/trace-sampling_test.ts (new), ENVIRONMENT.md, prd.json
- Notes: P2 observability/cost. Mostly a policy story but wired the actual sampling knob so it isn't doc-only. (1) Retention/rotation: pinned Docker json-file driver in docker-compose.coolify.yml (max-size 10m x max-file 5 = 50 MiB/container hard local cap, oldest dropped on roll-over) as the enforced on-box ceiling; OBSERVABILITY.md documents that plus 30-day hot / 90-day warm aggregator tiers. (2) Edge trace sampling: added traceSampleRate()/shouldSampleTrace() to observability.ts driven by EDGE_TRACE_SAMPLE_RATE (prod 0.1 set in compose, mirrors frontend Sentry tracesSampleRate). Deterministic FNV-1a hash of the per-request correlationId so a request's access line + all its timed() spans are kept/dropped together (coherent trace). Applied in access-log.ts (samples 2xx/3xx success lines, ALWAYS logs 4xx/5xx) and timed() (samples ok-outcome spans, always emits error spans). KEY DESIGN: recordMetric() business counters, exceptions, warns, and Sentry events stay 100% unsampled -- only the healthy-request firehose shrinks, so error-rate/audit/security signal is untouched. Env read per-call (not cached) -> testable + tunable without redeploy; non-numeric/blank fails OPEN to 1.0 so a typo never blinds observability; explicit rate 0 = drop-all emergency cap. (3) Cost budget: OBSERVABILITY.md has a derived-volume + ceiling table (<=5 GB/replica/mo, <=25 GB all replicas, <=50 USD/mo aggregator) with stated assumptions + a tune-the-rate-first review cadence. Env documented in ENVIRONMENT.md + .env.example. Verification: src/tests/trace-sampling_test.ts 9 pass (parse/clamp/fail-open, deterministic, ~0.1 fraction +/-5%, unkeyed-kept, rate-0 drop-all); deno check on observability.ts/access-log.ts/test + src/main.ts clean; deno lint clean; frontend npx tsc --noEmit exit 0 (no frontend files touched).

## US-594: Build a sold-comp, grade-banded pricing engine
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: services/edge-functions/src/lib/grade-band-pricing.ts (new), services/edge-functions/src/routes/flipdesk-pricing.ts (POST /price), services/edge-functions/src/tests/grade-band-pricing_test.ts (new, 9 tests), src/hooks/use-ebay.ts (useGradeBandedPrice), src/components/flipdesk/ebay-comps-panel.tsx (SoldCompRecommendation block), src/pages/flipdesk/composer.tsx (pass grade), prd.json
- Notes: The three ingredients already existed but in isolation — sold-comps.ts (US-542) returned realized comps but condition-blind; condition-value.ts (US-610) grade-banded but off ACTIVE asks; sell-through.ts (US-623) was a velocity heuristic. New grade-band-pricing.ts unifies them: realized sales (eBay Insights -> seller's private sales) are the primary basis, grade-positioned within the realized [p25,median,p75] band (positionRealizedByGrade), with sell-through folded in and comp-set source/count + confidence carried on every recommendation. Active asks are only a clearly-flagged fallback (soldBacked=false, dampened confidence). Pure core (positionRealizedByGrade/assembleRecommendation) is unit-tested without eBay/DB; thin I/O wrapper recommendGradeBandedPrice fetches comps then assembles. Endpoint POST /api/flipdesk/pricing/price (tenant-scoped via workspaceOwnerId, US-268). UI: EbayCompsPanel now shows a sold-comp recommendation card (price, range, basis badge, confidence %, sell-through label/days, comp count, Use-as-target-price) above the active-comp grid. Verified: deno check + deno lint + 9 new deno tests pass; frontend tsc --noEmit, npm run build, eslint all clean.
- Timestamp: 2026-06-12

- Notes:
  - The signal is deliberately SEPARATE from (a) the condition grade and (b) the photo-tamper check (image_authenticity, US-336-338). The prompt tells the model to ignore photo editing/screenshots and condition/wear, judging brand authenticity only. AC #2 satisfied.
  - "Premium add-on" modeled WITHOUT rebuilding billing precedence: gated to the paid Premium/Express tiers (whose higher charge covers it) + opt-in + feature flag. Standard grades never include it. Server re-enforces tier+flag regardless of client.
  - counterfeit_risk is derived deterministically in code (deriveCounterfeitRisk), not trusted to the model. limitations is always the fixed AUTHENTICITY_LIMITATIONS constant. AC #3 (confidence + limitations disclosed) satisfied on owner + public surfaces.
  - Pipeline runs the add-on INSIDE the existing withImageBufferSlot closure to reuse resident base64 (keeps the memory gate as the concurrency bound). Best-effort: a failure resolves null so a flaky add-on never fails a paid grade. Rare quality-gate abstention wastes one call but charges nothing extra.
  - Public view exposes only coarse buyer-safe fields (addon_included, confidence_label, counterfeit_risk, summary, limitations); raw red_flags stay server-side (a wrong public counterfeit claim would be harmful) — mirrors the image_authenticity.tells treatment.
  - PRE-EXISTING FAILURES (NOT this story): src/tests/plan-gate_test.ts + src/tests/rls-guard_test.ts fail on a clean tree (git stash confirmed) — introduced by US-600 / migration 00171 (rls-guard flags a tenant table missing restrictive RLS). Needs a follow-up; out of scope for US-601.
  - Verified: deno check src/main.ts, deno lint, deno test (ai-authenticity + grade-pricing 25 pass; full suite 967 pass / only the 2 pre-existing US-600 failures remain), npx tsc --noEmit, npm run build, eslint.

## US-602: Add reseller benchmarking / community insights
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: supabase/migrations/00173_community_benchmarks.sql (new), src/lib/community-benchmarks.ts (new), src/pages/flipdesk/community-insights.tsx (new), src/routes/index.tsx, src/components/dashboard/sidebar.tsx, services/edge-functions/src/lib/schema-version.ts, prd.json
- Notes: Cross-tenant anonymized analytics. Unlike every other analytics RPC (SECURITY INVOKER, single-tenant), community_benchmarks is SECURITY DEFINER because it must aggregate across ALL sellers. Privacy is earned via k-anonymity: every returned brand/category/peer cohort requires >= 5 distinct sellers; the only seller-specific values are the caller's own (auth.uid()). Peer comparison exposes cohort median + caller percentile only. Re-derives items_full's per-item facts (latest listing/sale via lateral joins) over all users since the security_invoker items_full view can't cross tenants. GOTCHA: percentile_cont returns double precision -> round(x::numeric, n) (round(double,int) doesn't exist in PG). Verified with supabase db reset + live RPC smoke-test (returns valid empty-state shape, no error).

## US-686: iOS: Auto-apply high-confidence AI extract with a reversible review entry
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: ios/GradeThread/AIExtract/AIExtractView.swift; ios/GradeThread/AIExtract/AIExtractStore.swift; ios/GradeThread/AIExtract/AIFillReview.swift (new); ios/GradeThread/AIExtract/AIFillReviewSheet.swift (new); ios/GradeThread/AIExtract/AIItemFieldWriter.swift (new); ios/GradeThread/Inventory/ItemCanvas/ItemCanvasView.swift; ios/GradeThreadTests/AIExtractTests.swift; prd.json; scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- Notes: Replaced the blocking AIExtract review fullScreenCover with an auto-apply flow. After the extract resolves, AIExtractView captures a pre-fill snapshot (for undo), writes the high-confidence (>=0.8) fields + measurements via a new shared AIItemFieldWriter (moved the inventory_items column mapping + ai_field_sources bookkeeping out of the view), registers an AIFillReview into a transient @Observable AIFillReviewStore.shared keyed by itemId, then completes straight to the item canvas (preserving US-682 nav + title seeding). The canvas shows a reversible "AI filled N fields — review" banner that opens the new AIFillReviewSheet: per-field keep/undo (restores the snapshot's previous value via an explicit-null RevertUpdate encoder, since the sparse FieldUpdate can't null a column), inline opt-in for low-confidence suggestions (incl. Live Text fallback at 0.4), keep/drop measurements, and an "Undo AI fill" that reverts everything + clears ai_field_sources. Low-confidence-only results write nothing but still surface for opt-in. Undo of seeded title intentionally not included (title stays findable per US-682). Tests: confidence partition + previousValue capture, nil-when-not-ready, entry-point label, store register/clear, explicit-null revert encoding. iOS can't be compiled on Windows (Swift/xcodebuild is macOS-only) — relying on iOS CI (macOS runners) to validate the build; no web/edge files touched. Project is XcodeGen (directory-based sources) so the 3 new files are auto-included.
- Timestamp: 2026-06-13

## US-711: Shopify order + inventory webhooks → sale capture & cross-platform auto-delist
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed:
  - services/edge-functions/src/lib/shopify-graphql.ts (registerShopifyWebhooks + SHOPIFY_WEBHOOK_TOPICS via webhookSubscriptionCreate)
  - services/edge-functions/src/lib/shopify-client.ts (verifyWebhookHmac, getShopifyWebhookUrl, ShopifyOrder.cancelledAt)
  - services/edge-functions/src/lib/shopify-orders.ts (NEW: shared ingestShopifyOrder/applyShopifyOrderStatus/handleShopifyOrderEvent/parseShopifyWebhookOrder/refundStatusForOrder)
  - services/edge-functions/src/lib/webhook-idempotency.ts (add "shopify" to provider union)
  - services/edge-functions/src/routes/flipdesk-shopify.ts (register webhooks on connect; pull now reuses ingestShopifyOrder)
  - services/edge-functions/src/routes/flipdesk-webhooks.ts (POST /shopify receiver + processShopifyWebhookEvent + product-update/owner-resolve helpers)
  - services/edge-functions/src/tests/{shopify-client_test,shopify-orders_test,shopify-graphql_test}.ts
- Notes:
  - HMAC: Shopify webhooks sign the RAW body with HMAC-SHA256 (base64) in X-Shopify-Hmac-Sha256 — distinct from the OAuth callback's hex-over-querystring HMAC. Verify BEFORE trusting the body or the X-Shopify-Shop-Domain header; only Shopify can produce a valid sig, so the domain header is trusted post-verify to resolve the owner (US-472 pattern).
  - Idempotency: X-Shopify-Webhook-Id via claimWebhookEvent("shopify",…) tri-state; plus sale dedupe by listing_id and payout dedupe by ref → idempotent on shop order id. No migration: processed_webhook_events.provider is a free-text column.
  - Refactored the manual /listings/pull per-order loop to call the shared ingestShopifyOrder so webhook + pull can't drift (behavior identical; per-order product lookup instead of one batch).
  - Receiver returns 200 fast and dispatches async (Shopify penalizes slow handlers / expects 2xx within ~5s).
  - Verify: deno lint + deno check (incl. main.ts graph) clean; new unit tests pass; full edge suite 982 passed / 2 failed — the 2 are the documented pre-existing plan-gate + rls-guard failures on clean main, unrelated to this change.
- Timestamp: 2026-06-13

## US-713: Depop connector — OAuth 2.0 + PKCE connection + token refresh (flagged until approved)
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: services/edge-functions/src/lib/depop-client.ts (new); services/edge-functions/src/routes/flipdesk-depop.ts (new); services/edge-functions/src/lib/marketplace-adapters/depop.ts (wired connect/refresh); services/edge-functions/src/main.ts (mount); services/edge-functions/src/routes/flipdesk-ebay.ts (shared refresh cron); services/edge-functions/src/lib/schema-version.ts (00175); supabase/migrations/00175_oauth_states_code_verifier.sql (new); services/edge-functions/src/tests/depop-client_test.ts (new); services/edge-functions/src/tests/marketplace-adapters_test.ts; prd.json; scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- Notes:
  - CONNECTION half only (publish/order-sync is US-714, still typed-501). depop-client.ts: OAuth 2.0 Authorization Code + PKCE (S256). Unlike eBay/Shopify, Depop mandates PKCE, so the random code_verifier must survive the start→callback round-trip — added migration 00175 adding a nullable `code_verifier` column to the existing single-use oauth_states row (no new table; eBay/Shopify rows leave it null). PKCE helpers are pure Web Crypto (base64url SHA-256), unit-tested against the RFC 7636 Appendix B vector.
  - Tokens (access+refresh) stored encrypted in marketplace_connections (marketplace='depop', already a valid listing_platform enum value — no enum migration) AES-GCM-bound to user_id as AAD (US-352), mirroring the eBay path. getDepopAccessToken refreshes inline near expiry (60s window), persists a rotated refresh token when Depop returns one, and on a PERMANENT auth failure (invalid_grant/4xx) deactivates + stores DEPOP_RECONNECT_MESSAGE (US-463 classifier), TRANSIENT (5xx/429/network) leaves is_active=true.
  - GATING (AC#3): isDepopEnabled() = DEPOP_ENABLED flag (true/1/yes/on) AND creds present; default OFF. Every flipdesk-depop endpoint + the adapter connect/refresh 503 while off → no fake connect flow before partner access is live (ties US-458/US-718). The route is mounted but inert until an operator flips the flag post-approval.
  - SHARED refresh worker (AC#2): rather than a new Coolify scheduled task, the existing eBay /oauth/refresh cron now also calls refreshExpiringDepopConnections() (gated → no-op while disabled). Response gains a `depop` summary.
  - AC#4 (sandbox round-trip) is the human-gated step pending Depop partner access (US-712). Everything an agent can do is built + unit-tested against the flow: PKCE RFC vector, full multi-seller scope set in the consent URL, the enabled gate (flag XOR creds), and the permanent/transient classifier.
  - Env (Coolify, all only read when enabled): DEPOP_ENABLED, DEPOP_CLIENT_ID, DEPOP_CLIENT_SECRET, DEPOP_REDIRECT_URI, optional DEPOP_AUTH_URL/DEPOP_TOKEN_URL/DEPOP_SCOPES.
  - Verify: deno lint (5 files), deno check src/main.ts (full graph), deno test depop-client + marketplace-adapters (10/10) + schema-version (9/9), frontend tsc -b — all green. The only edge test failures are the documented pre-existing rls-guard (ai_usage_events/waitlist_entries lacking RLS) + plan-gate (SUPABASE_URL-not-set in isolation) ones on clean main, unrelated to this change.
- Timestamp: 2026-06-13

  - No DB migration needed — reuses the existing listings schema (listing_url, listing_status, is_active, draft_id from US-149).

  - Phased rollout is a one-line flip in selectors.js (enabled:true) + bump version/lastVerified once a platform's flow is verified against the live site. Disabled platforms degrade to a clear 'list manually' message; broken selectors fail loudly (probe() before fill).
  - Auto-submit is intentionally OFF (category/size/condition pickers vary too much + the seller is responsible for the final review per clickwrap) — the extension prefills and the seller submits.
  - Verify: npx tsc -b + eslint (changed files) + vitest (5/5) + npm run build all green; edge deno check (full graph) + deno lint + tenant-isolation/marketplace-adapters tests green.

## US-835: System prompt + output guardrails: scope confinement & prompt-injection resistance
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: services/edge-functions/src/lib/support-assistant-engine.ts (hardened SUPPORT_SYSTEM_PROMPT + new guardAssistantOutput/GUARD_REFUSAL), services/edge-functions/src/routes/support-assistant.ts (buffer output, run guard, record abuse event, guarded flag on done), services/edge-functions/src/tests/support-output-guard_test.ts (new), prd.json
- Notes: The system prompt is now the explicit tier-1 scope policy — role, KB+own-data-only sourcing, allowed-topic taxonomy, the untrusted-input rule (all user/pasted text is DATA, never instructions; 'ignore previous instructions' is not obeyed), an enumerated non-disclosure list (system prompt, tools, schema, other tenants, internal config, model/provider details), and refusal+always-offer-escalation. guardAssistantOutput() is a deterministic, model-free post-filter on the model's FINAL answer: matches internal tool names, internal table/schema names, and tight infra/config/model regexes; on a hit it DISCARDS the offending text, returns GUARD_REFUSAL, and classifies abuse as scope_probe (tool/schema leak) or prompt_injection (system/model/infra leak). Route change: the model's text is BUFFERED (live deltas suppressed) so the guard runs before ANYTHING reaches the client — a leak is never returned even transiently; a trip calls recordAbuseEvent (US-831, severity high) with the conversation id and the offending content is not persisted. SSE contract preserved (clients still accumulate delta.text; done event gained a `guarded` boolean). No DB migration -> EXPECTED_SCHEMA_VERSION untouched. Verified: deno check + deno lint clean on the 3 files; 13 new guard/prompt tests + 9 existing engine tests = 22 passed / 0 failed.

  Escalation: there is NO user-initiated escalation endpoint (escalation_trigger 'user' is reserved per support-escalation.ts) — the 'Talk to a human' button sends a canned "I'd like to talk to a human support agent, please." which reliably trips the model's escalate_to_human tool (engine system prompt: escalate when "the user asks for a person"). The done event returns escalated/status, flipping the UI to the human-handled banner; once human-handled the composer placeholder changes and the Talk-to-human button hides.
  Realtime: admin replies (admin-support.ts reply/resolve) notify via the notifications table (in supabase_realtime publication, 00007). The widget subscribes to notifications INSERT filtered to the user (mirrors notification-center.tsx) and, while open with an active conversation, refetches that conversation's transcript so a human_agent reply appears live without refresh. support_messages itself is NOT in the realtime publication, so notifications is the correct live channel.
  Tier gate: subscription_status in {active,trialing} -> chat; otherwise an UpsellPanel (See plans -> usePlanPickerStore.show()); a runtime 403 not_subscribed (e.g. a workspace member whose owner billing differs) also routes to the upsell. Server remains authoritative.

## US-854: Wire and document social fan-out for published content
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: docs/CONTENT_PUBLISHING.md (new), docs/CONTENT_SCHEDULER.md, prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- Notes: Closes the "last mile" of the Content Engine Activation epic. The runtime path was already fully built across prior stories: content-blog.ts /publish and content-social.ts /publish call dispatchContentWebhook (lib/content-webhook.ts), which resolves the target URL from content_settings (make_webhook_blog/_social_long/_social_short), HMAC-SHA256-signs the body with CONTENT_WEBHOOK_SIGNING_SECRET (X-Content-Signature), POSTs with X-Content-Event/X-Content-Format headers, retries 3x (0/5/30s, 10s timeout), logs every attempt to content_webhook_log, and on terminal failure alerts ops (CONTENT_ALERT_WEBHOOK -> MONITOR_ALERT_WEBHOOK fallback, US-487) while staying retryable. Social fires one event per non-empty body (long->LinkedIn/FB scenario, short->X/Threads scenario), same post id across both. UTM is channel-NEUTRAL at the edge (buildSocialCtaUrl -> utm_source=social&utm_medium=social&utm_campaign=<slug>); the per-channel utm_source rewrite is a downstream (Make.com) responsibility.
  This story's deliverable was the missing CONTENT contract doc: new docs/CONTENT_PUBLISHING.md documents (AC1) field-by-field payload contracts for blog.published + social.published(long/short) incl. types/nullability; transport headers + how to verify X-Content-Signature downstream; (AC3) the per-channel utm_source rewrite table (linkedin/twitter/facebook/threads/pinterest/instagram) and that the edge emits neutral utm_source=social for the scenario to rewrite; (AC4) events/payloads/secrets (CONTENT_WEBHOOK_SIGNING_SECRET, CONTENT_ALERT_WEBHOOK, the three DB-stored target URLs), an 8-step "How to add a channel" runbook, the /webhooks/test test-fire (mirrors real payload + test:true so Make.com maps every field), retry endpoint, content_webhook_log observability; and (AC2) an operator go-live checklist for taking ONE live channel end-to-end. Verified all documented API paths against main.ts mounts (/api/content/settings/webhooks/test|/:logId/retry, /api/content/scheduler/tick) and payload field lists against the actual dispatch call sites. Cross-linked the doc from CONTENT_SCHEDULER.md.
  AC2 (a real publish reaching a live social channel) is inherently operator/human-gated: it needs a Make.com scenario built + a social channel OAuth-connected, neither of which exists in this repo or CI. The doc provides the exact, ordered steps to satisfy it (incl. the test-fire dry run that proves the wire end-to-end before the first live post). No code change was required or appropriate — the dispatch path is complete and tested; bolting on a fake "channel" would not satisfy the AC and would add dead code.
- Verification: npm run build green (tsc -b + vite + 32-route prerender). Docs-only change; no tsc/lint/migration impact (schema unchanged at 00192).

Visibility guard (AC#4): it first confirms the cert via /api/content/public/certificates/:id (which hard-filters flagged/private reports per US-484/US-268) — a miss returns a 404 no-op script so nothing renders. DELIBERATELY chose script over iframe: the zone ships X-Frame-Options: DENY + CSP frame-ancestors 'none' for clickjacking defense, and Cloudflare Pages _headers JOINS duplicate header values with a comma (confirmed via CF community/docs) rather than letting a per-path rule override, so a same-origin iframe can't be framed cross-site without weakening app-wide security — a script injects into the host DOM and is immune to framing. verified.ts: certBadgeEmbedHtml + certBadgeEmbedText now carry ?s=embed (were missing it); added certificateShareUrl(), certBadgeScriptUrl(), certBadgeScriptEmbed(). Cert page embed card now offers three copy-paste snippets: Script (websites/blogs) + HTML <img> (marketplaces) + plain text.
