- Files changed: services/edge-functions/src/lib/referral-rewards.ts (new), services/edge-functions/src/lib/referrals.ts, services/edge-functions/src/routes/referrals.ts, services/edge-functions/src/routes/admin-growth.ts, services/edge-functions/src/routes/content-public.ts, services/edge-functions/src/lib/schema-version.ts, services/edge-functions/src/tests/referral-rewards_test.ts (new), supabase/migrations/00195_referral_leaderboard_optin.sql (new), src/components/referral/top-referrers.tsx (new), src/pages/referral-leaderboard.tsx (new), src/pages/referrals.tsx, src/routes/index.tsx, src/lib/seo/public-routes.ts, src/prerender/entry-server.tsx, src/types/database.ts, prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- Notes: Verified the existing loop first (per the story's double-grant warning): credits already apply end-to-end (grant_grade_credits RPC -> users.grade_credit_balance -> debit_grade_credits in grading), but the GRANT was 100% manual (super-admin only). Closed the loop by (1) extracting the credit-grant into ONE shared race-safe fn grantReferralReward() in lib/referrals.ts: CLAIMS the row (status flip with .neq('granted')) BEFORE moving balances so two concurrent callers can't double-grant, rolls the claim back to 'qualified' if the RPC fails, refuses if either party is suspended (US-802 safeguard), notifies both parties best-effort. (2) maybeQualifyReferral now AUTO-GRANTS each freshly-qualified referral (was stopping at 'qualified'); blocked/transient results leave it at 'qualified' for the admin queue. (3) Admin grant route refactored to call the same shared fn (override remains, still super-admin+step-up+audited). Reward sizes moved to a tiny leaf module referral-rewards.ts so the public surfaces import the constants without pulling notify/email. (4) /referrals/me now returns credits {per_referral, earned (granted*5), pending (in-flight*5)} + leaderboard {enabled, display_name}; new PUT /referrals/leaderboard manages the opt-in (alias required to go public). (5) Public PII-free GET /api/content/public/referral-leaderboard.json — only opted-in users (referral_leaderboard_enabled) with >=1 granted referral, returns alias + count + credits only (no email/name/id); ranking via pure unit-tested rankReferrers(). (6) Frontend: reusable <TopReferrers> component, new public /leaderboard page (registered in PUBLIC_ROUTES + entry-server + lastmod so the prerender + sitemap + route guards stay green — prerendered shell, list loads client-side like /status & /verified), referrals page shows earned-vs-pending credits + opt-in controls + a top-5 board. Migration 00195 adds users.referral_leaderboard_enabled (default false) + referral_display_name (no public RLS; served via service-role edge like verified profiles).
- Verification: npx tsc --noEmit clean; eslint clean on changed FE files; deno check + deno lint clean on changed edge files; npm run build:locked green (38 prerendered pages incl /leaderboard); npm test 688/688 pass (added 5 rankReferrers cases; fixed PUBLIC_ROUTES + prerender guards by registering /leaderboard); deno test 1231/0 pass (edge); npm run verify:db green (all 195 migrations apply from zero in Docker). schema-version bumped to 00195.
- Timestamp: 2026-06-13

## US-869: Scheduler + webhook observability and stall/failure alerting
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed: supabase/migrations/00198_content_scheduler_runs.sql (new), services/edge-functions/src/lib/schema-version.ts (bump 00197→00198), services/edge-functions/src/routes/content-scheduler.ts (recordSchedulerRun heartbeat at every /tick exit), services/edge-functions/src/routes/jobs-content-watchdog.ts (new: pure evaluateWatchdog + handleContentWatchdogCron), services/edge-functions/src/lib/email.ts (new sendContentWatchdogAlertEmail), services/edge-functions/src/main.ts (register POST /api/jobs/content-watchdog), services/edge-functions/src/tests/jobs-content-watchdog_test.ts (new, 7 cases), services/edge-functions/src/tests/email-category_test.ts (add sender), LAUNCH_CHECKLIST.md + services/edge-functions/COOLIFY.md (cron entry, 0 */3 * * *), prd.json
- Notes: New content_scheduler_runs table is a durable per-tick heartbeat (outcome success/skip/error; admin-read RLS, service-role writes bypass RLS). Watchdog flags (a) no NON-ERROR tick in trailing 3h — a healthy "skip" (cadence met / paused) still counts as alive so the hourly cron doesn't false-alarm — and (b) >25% content_webhook_log failures over 24h, guarded by a 4-attempt minimum sample. Owner alert reuses the existing categorized email outbox (sendContentWatchdogAlertEmail, US-801 durable retry) routing to CONTENT_ALERT_EMAIL→MONITOR_ALERT_EMAIL→SMTP_ADMIN_EMAIL, plus optional CONTENT_ALERT_WEBHOOK→MONITOR_ALERT_WEBHOOK; an alert that reaches no channel is recorded as undelivered (not silent). Uses shared FLIPDESK_INTERNAL_JOB_SECRET (default requireJobSecret) like the other Coolify crons, NOT the content scenario's CONTENT_INTERNAL_JOB_SECRET. evaluateWatchdog is pure for unit testing. Suggested migration number 00146 in the AC was stale (latest was 00197) — used 00198. Verified: deno check+lint, 1240 edge tests + 7 new watchdog tests pass, verify:db applies 00198 from zero, npm run build:locked, 700 frontend tests pass.
- Timestamp: 2026-06-13

  - prd.json
- Notes:
  - Complements (does NOT duplicate) the existing live /api/admin/fraud aggregate: that computes on-the-fly each request with no memory; this is the DURABLE, triageable queue, and its headline signal (cross-account phash photo reuse) is something the live dashboard does not compute.
  - AC4 cross-tenant safety: evidence carries only ids + hash + distance + counts. assertSafeEvidence() enforces a key allow-list and rejects anything resembling image bytes / signed storage URLs; unit-tested. The detector only ever touches phash text + submission->user-id maps, never image bytes.
  - subject_user_id (NOT user_id) is deliberate: the rls-guard auto-discovers `\buser_id\b` and would misclassify this admin-only safety table as user-owned tenant data. It's service-role-only (deny-all RLS) so users can't read signals raised about themselves.
  - Actions reuse existing audited endpoints (suspend = /api/admin/users/:id/suspend, void grade = /api/admin/moderation/:id/reject); only the signal's own triage state lives on the safety router. Resolving (actioned/dismissed/open) requires super_admin MFA step-up + a reason, matching the bar of the destructive actions it represents.
  - Idempotent scan: dedupe_key unique; re-run upserts evidence/last_seen without duplicating or reopening resolved signals (status not in the upsert payload).
  - Verified: deno check/lint clean, full edge suite 1415 passed/0 failed, tsc --noEmit clean, build:locked 41 pages, vitest 772 passed, verify:db re-applied 212 migrations from zero (28.7s).
  - Left the concurrent agent's uncommitted grading WIP (ai-grading.ts / grading-pipeline.ts / grading-zoom_test.ts) untouched.

## US-897: Marketplace connection health console (all tenants)
- Status: COMPLETE
- Files changed:
  - services/edge-functions/src/lib/marketplace-health.ts (new: pure deriveHealth + token-sanitizing toConnectionHealth DTO)
  - services/edge-functions/src/routes/admin-marketplace-connections.ts (new: GET list+summary, POST /:id/refresh, POST /:id/flag-reconnect)
  - services/edge-functions/src/main.ts (import + mount /api/admin/marketplace-connections)

  - src/layouts/admin-layout.tsx (new 'Marketplace' sidebar group + PlugZap icon)
  - src/routes/index.tsx (lazy import + route)
  - prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- Notes:
  * No migration: marketplace_connections (00008) already had every column the AC needs (token_expires_at, refresh_error, last_synced_at, last_refresh_attempt_at, account_handle, is_active) — so no EXPECTED_SCHEMA_VERSION bump, no verify:db, no rls-guard impact.
  * AC2 (tokens never returned) is enforced two ways: CONNECTION_SELECT never selects the *_encrypted columns, and the pure toConnectionHealth() sanitizer builds the DTO field-by-field. Unit test feeds a row WITH the secrets and asserts they're absent from the DTO + serialized JSON.
  * Derived health order: refresh_error -> 'error' (wins even if is_active=false, since a permanent eBay failure deactivates AND stores the error); else !is_active -> 'inactive'; else token within 7d / unknown -> 'expiring'; else 'healthy'. Summary = 4 mutually-exclusive head:count queries (sum = total).
  * AC5 reuse: refresh calls getUserAccessToken (ebay) / getDepopAccessToken (depop) — the existing AES-GCM refresh-and-persist path — instead of reimplementing crypto. Caveat (documented): those operate on the owner's active connection for that marketplace and only rotate when the token is <60s from expiry; for a valid token the action confirms validity. Non-ebay/depop marketplaces return 400 -> use flag-for-reconnect.
  * flag-reconnect soft-sets refresh_error (so it reads 'error' in the console) WITHOUT revoking — listings keep syncing until expiry — and notifies the owner via notifyUser(type:'system', link:/dashboard/flipdesk/marketplaces). A later successful refresh clears the marker.
  * Mutations are super_admin + requireStepUp + writeAuditLog; frontend is step-up-aware (catches STEP_UP_REQUIRED -> MfaStepUpDialog -> retry). Cross-tenant reads are intentional (admin oversight surface), documented in the route header re: CLAUDE.md US-268.
  * Concurrent Ralph agent was mid-flight in the grading subsystem (US-1034 model-comparison WIP + committed US-1036); deliberately picked US-897 (cold FlipDesk/marketplace subsystem) to avoid file conflicts. Staged ONLY my files at commit.
  * Verified: deno check + lint clean; full edge suite 1486 passed/0 failed; tsc --noEmit clean; vitest 772 passed; build:locked 41 pages.
- Timestamp: 2026-06-14
